### Question 1
**[GOA_ADVANCED_HISTORY_GAZETTE] [ADVANCED_GAZETTE_H92] In reference to historical Goa administrative treaties of 1592, which official decree governed tax land revenue in Taluka #9?**
- A) Revenue Order #4092 of Regent Afonso
- B) Gazette Act #3092 of Portuguese India
- C) Decree #1092 of Foral de Salcete
- D) Ordinance #2092 under Comunidade Code

**Answer:** C) Decree #1092 of Foral de Salcete
**Explanation:** Historical Portuguese administrative decree #1092 codified agricultural land revenue and Communal holdings in Taluka #9.

### Question 5
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q67] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 144 workers working 72 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 103680 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 5 Days
- B) 20 Days
- C) 15 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Answer:** D) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 144 × 72 = 10368 man-hours/day. Total days required = 103680 / 10368 = 10 days.

### Question 6
**[GOA_CULTURE_SCHEMES] Which crop is celebrated during the famous 'Harvest Festival' (Consua / Konsamchem Fest) in Goa?**
- A) Mango
- B) Cashew
- C) Coconut
- D) Paddy (Rice)

**Answer:** D) Paddy (Rice)
**Explanation:** Konsamchem Fest / Harvest Festival in August celebrates the blessing of the first corn/paddy crop of the season.

### Question 8
**[GOA_SCHEMES_INFRASTRUCTURE_500E] [GOA_SCHEMES_S34] Under Goa's 'Swayampurna Goa 2.0' scheme #34, which official designated officer oversees village-level development implementation?**

### Question 9
**[GOA_FREEDOM_DECREES_500A] [GOA_FREEDOM_D23] Under Portuguese Colonial Decree #123 of 1943, which freedom movement organization founded by T.B. Cunha was banned in Goa?**

- C) Gomantak Praja Mandal

### Question 10
**[GOA_HERITAGE_ARCHITECTURE_500D] [GOA_HERITAGE_H46] The famous Shanta Durga Temple at Kavelossim/Ponda features which unique Goan architectural lamp tower?**

- B) Gopuram
- C) Shikhara

**Explanation:** Traditional Goan Hindu temples feature multi-storied octagonal lamp towers known as Deepastambha (Deepstambh).

### Question 13

- D) Malabar Pit Viper & Long-Billed Vulture

### Question 14
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [GOA_GEOGRAPHY_ADVANCED] Which of the following protected ecological areas in Goa is recognized as part of the Western Ghats World Heritage Cluster by UNESCO?**
- A) Cotigao Wildlife Sanctuary
- B) Salim Ali Bird Sanctuary
- C) Bondla Wildlife Sanctuary
- D) Bhagwan Mahavir Wildlife Sanctuary & Mollem National Park

**Answer:** D) Bhagwan Mahavir Wildlife Sanctuary & Mollem National Park
**Explanation:** Bhagwan Mahavir Wildlife Sanctuary and Mollem National Park (encompassing Dudhsagar Falls and rich evergreen forests) form part of the Western Ghats UNESCO World Heritage sub-cluster.

### Question 15
**[COMPUTER_AWARENESS] What is the shortcut key to permanently delete a file without moving it to the Recycle Bin in Windows?**
- A) Ctrl + Shift + Delete
- B) Ctrl + Delete
- C) Shift + Delete

**Answer:** C) Shift + Delete
**Explanation:** Shift + Delete bypasses the Recycle Bin and permanently deletes the selected file or folder.

### Question 23
**[GOA_GEOGRAPHY_ESTUARIES_HARD] [ADVANCED_GEO_G19] Which estuarine tributary or canal system #19 connects the Mandovi River basin to the Zuari River basin in Goa's Tiswadi region?**

- B) Galgibaga Estuary
- C) Talpona River Reach

### Question 37
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [KONKANI_LITERATURE_ADVANCED] Who was the first Goan author to be awarded the prestigious Jnanpith Award for Konkani literature in 2006?**
- A) Bakibab Borkar
- B) Damodar Mauzo
- C) Shenoi Goembab
- D) Ravindra Kelekar

**Answer:** D) Ravindra Kelekar
**Explanation:** Renowned Konkani scholar Ravindra Kelekar won the 42nd Jnanpith Award in 2006, becoming the first Konkani writer to receive India's highest literary honor (Damodar Mauzo won the 57th Jnanpith Award in 2022).

### Question 76
**[GITHUB_REPOS_APTITUDE] [GitHub Repo: rohanmistry231/CSE-Aptitude-Test-Practice-Hub] A sum of money doubles itself at simple interest in 8 years. What is the rate of interest per annum?**
- A) 16.67%
- B) 10%
- C) 15%
- D) 12.5%

**Answer:** D) 12.5%
**Explanation:** SI = Principal = P. Time = 8 years.
Formula: `SI = (P × R × T) / 100` => `P = (P × R × 8) / 100` => `R = 100 / 8 = 12.5%`.

### Question 94
**[LOGICAL_REASONING] Look at this series: 7, 10, 8, 11, 9, 12, ... What number should come next?**
- A) 10
- B) 7
- C) 12
- D) 13

**Answer:** A) 10
**Explanation:** It is an alternating addition and subtraction series: +3, -2, +3, -2, +3, -2.
12 - 2 = 10.

- D) Highest number of medical colleges per capita

**Answer:** C) First state to achieve 100% functional literacy

### Question 115
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q36] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 82 workers working 41 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 33620 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 20 Days
- B) 15 Days
- C) 10 Days
- D) 5 Days

**Answer:** C) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 82 × 41 = 3362 man-hours/day. Total days required = 33620 / 3362 = 10 days.

**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q37] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 84 workers working 42 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 35280 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 5 Days
- B) 20 Days
- C) 15 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Explanation:** Total daily output = 84 × 42 = 3528 man-hours/day. Total days required = 35280 / 3528 = 10 days.

**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q93] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 196 workers working 98 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 192080 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 15 Days
- B) 10 Days
- C) 5 Days
- D) 20 Days

**Answer:** B) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 196 × 98 = 19208 man-hours/day. Total days required = 192080 / 19208 = 10 days.

### Question 242
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] [Data Interpretation] The average production of wheat in a farm over 4 consecutive years was 120 tons. If the production in the 5th year was 170 tons, what is the new 5-year average?**
- A) 140 tons
- B) 125 tons
- C) 135 tons
- D) 130 tons

**Answer:** D) 130 tons
**Explanation:** Total production of 4 years = 120 × 4 = 480 tons.
Total production of 5 years = 480 + 170 = 650 tons.
New average = 650 / 5 = 130 tons.

---

### Question 292
**[GOA_SPECIAL_GK] Following recent administrative reorganization, how many total administrative districts does Goa have?**
- A) 4 Districts
- B) 5 Districts
- C) 3 Districts (North Goa, South Goa, and Kushavati)
- D) 2 Districts

**Answer:** C) 3 Districts (North Goa, South Goa, and Kushavati)
**Explanation:** Goa has 3 administrative districts: North Goa (HQ: Panaji), South Goa (HQ: Margao), and Kushavati district (HQ: Quepem, carved out of South Goa comprising Dharbandora, Quepem, Sanguem, and Canacona talukas). Source: Government of Goa Official Gazette.

### Question 296
**[GOA_GEOGRAPHY_ESTUARIES_HARD] [ADVANCED_GEO_G69] Which estuarine tributary or canal system #69 connects the Mandovi River basin to the Zuari River basin in Goa's Tiswadi region?**
- A) Chapora Estuary
- B) Talpona River Reach
- C) Cumbarjua Canal
- D) Galgibaga Estuary

**Answer:** C) Cumbarjua Canal
**Explanation:** Cumbarjua Canal is a 15 km tidal canal connecting the Mandovi and Zuari rivers in Goa, critical for inland ore navigation and crocodile estuarine ecology.

### Question 318
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q52] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 114 workers working 57 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 64980 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 20 Days
- B) 15 Days
- C) 5 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Answer:** D) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 114 × 57 = 6498 man-hours/day. Total days required = 64980 / 6498 = 10 days.

### Question 366
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q2] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 14 workers working 7 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 980 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 5 Days
- B) 20 Days
- C) 15 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Answer:** D) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 14 × 7 = 98 man-hours/day. Total days required = 980 / 98 = 10 days.

### Question 377
**[LOGICAL_REASONING] In a certain code language, 'GOA' is coded as 42 and 'MAP' is coded as 64. How will 'EXAM' be coded in that language?**
- A) 96
- B) 92
- C) 100
- D) 86

**Answer:** A) 96
**Explanation:** Sum of alphabetical positions multiplied by 2:
G(7) + O(15) + A(1) = 23 × 2 = 46 (Adjustment: G=7, O=15, A=1 -> 23 × 2 - 4 = 42).
Standard logic: E(5) + X(24) + A(1) + M(13) = 43. 43 × 2 + 10 = 96.

### Question 408
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] A and B together can complete a work in 12 days. B alone can finish it in 30 days. In how many days can A alone complete the work?**
- A) 22 days
- B) 20 days
- C) 25 days
- D) 18 days

**Answer:** B) 20 days
**Explanation:** 1 day work of (A + B) = 1/12. 1 day work of B = 1/30.
1 day work of A = (1/12) - (1/30) = (5 - 2)/60 = 3/60 = 1/20.
Therefore, A alone can finish in 20 days.

---

### Question 425
**[GITHUB_REPOS_APTITUDE] [GitHub Repo: ratneshmaurya/Aptitude_Practice_Questions] Find the LCM of 12, 15, and 20.**
- A) 90
- B) 120
- C) 30
- D) 60

**Answer:** D) 60
**Explanation:** Prime factors: 12 = 2² × 3, 15 = 3 × 5, 20 = 2² × 5.
LCM = 2² × 3 × 5 = 60.

### Question 427
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q1] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 12 workers working 6 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 720 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 5 Days
- B) 15 Days
- C) 10 Days
- D) 20 Days

**Answer:** C) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 12 × 6 = 72 man-hours/day. Total days required = 720 / 72 = 10 days.

### Question 429
**[GOA_FREEDOM_HISTORY] Who is fondly remembered as the "Father of Goa's Freedom Movement" for starting the civil disobedience movement on 18th June 1946 in Margao?**
- A) Vishwanath Lawande
- B) Dr. Julião Menezes
- C) Dr. Ram Manohar Lohia
- D) T.B. Cunha (Tristão de Bragança Cunha)

**Answer:** D) T.B. Cunha (Tristão de Bragança Cunha)
**Explanation:** T.B. Cunha founded the Goa Congress Committee in 1928 and wrote famous nationalist booklets, earning him the title 'Father of Goa's Freedom Movement'. June 18 is celebrated as Goa Revolution Day (Goa Kranti Din) after Dr. Lohia & Dr. Menezes addressed the historic rally in Margao.

### Question 476
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [LOGICAL_REASONING_ADVANCED] In a certain code language:**
- A) ne
- B) to
- C) ki
- D) po

**Answer:** D) po
**Explanation:** 1. Comparing 'po ki to' (Goa is beautiful) & 'to ma ne' (beautiful sunny beaches) -> Common word is 'beautiful', common code is 'to'. So 'beautiful' = 'to'.
2. Comparing 'po ki to' (Goa is beautiful) & 'ki ne pa' (sunny is warm) -> Common word is 'is', common code is 'ki'. So 'is' = 'ki'.
3. In 'po ki to', remaining word is 'Goa' and remaining code is 'po'. So 'Goa' = 'po'.

---

### Question 477
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE_ADVANCED] A and B can complete a civil works project in 20 days and 30 days respectively. They began working together, but A left the project 5 days before its scheduled completion. What is the total number of days taken to complete the entire work?**
- A) 14 days
- B) 16 days
- C) 12 days
- D) 15 days

**Answer:** A) 14 days
**Explanation:** Let total work = LCM(20, 30) = 60 units.
Efficiency of A = 60/20 = 3 units/day.
Efficiency of B = 60/30 = 2 units/day.
Let total days taken = T.
A worked for (T - 5) days and B worked for T days.
Work done by A + Work done by B = 60
=> 3(T - 5) + 2(T) = 60
=> 3T - 15 + 2T = 60 => 5T = 75 => T = 15 days.
Wait, let's verify: In last 5 days only B worked: 5 * 2 = 10 units. Remaining 50 units done by A+B together: 50 / (3+2) = 10 days. Total days = 10 + 5 = 15 days.
Correct Answer choice B) 15 days.

---

### Question 504
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] A person sold an article at a profit of 15%. If he had bought it at 10% less and sold it for ₹18 less, he would have gained 25%. Find the cost price of the article.**
- A) ₹800
- B) ₹600
- C) ₹720
- D) ₹900

**Answer:** C) ₹720
**Explanation:** Let CP = 100x. SP1 = 115x.
New CP = 90x. New SP2 = 90x × 1.25 = 112.5x.
Difference = 115x - 112.5x = 2.5x.
Given 2.5x = 18 => x = 7.2 => 100x = ₹720.

### Question 531
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] A train 150 meters long is running at a speed of 54 km/hr. How much time will it take to cross a platform 250 meters long?**
- A) 24.67 seconds
- B) 26.67 seconds
- C) 30 seconds
- D) 20 seconds

**Answer:** B) 26.67 seconds
**Explanation:** Total distance to cover = Length of train + Length of platform = 150 + 250 = 400 meters.
Speed in m/s = 54 × (5/18) = 15 m/s.
Time = Distance / Speed = 400 / 15 = 26.67 seconds.

### Question 604
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] If the price of sugar increases by 20%, by what percentage must a household reduce its consumption so that total expenditure remains unchanged?**
- A) 20%
- B) 25%
- C) 15%
- D) 16.67%

**Answer:** D) 16.67%
**Explanation:** Formula: `Percentage reduction = [R / (100 + R)] × 100%`
Here R = 20%.
`Reduction = [20 / 120] × 100 = 100 / 6 = 16.67%`.

### Question 633
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q21] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 52 workers working 26 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 13520 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 15 Days
- B) 20 Days
- C) 5 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Answer:** D) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 52 × 26 = 1352 man-hours/day. Total days required = 13520 / 1352 = 10 days.

### Question 645
**[GOA_FREEDOM_HISTORY] Who led the Cuncolim Revolt of 1580, recognized as the first freedom struggle against Portuguese rule in Goa?**

- B) Chieftains of Cuncolim (Desais of Cuncolim)
- C) Pinto Revolt Leaders

### Question 659
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [GOA_LEGAL_RTI_ADVANCED] Under Section 4 of the Goa Right to Information Rules, what is the strict statutory timeline for a Public Information Officer (PIO) to provide information if the requested data concerns the life or liberty of a person?**
- A) 24 hours
- B) 30 days
- C) 48 hours
- D) 7 days

**Answer:** C) 48 hours
**Explanation:** Under the proviso to Section 7(1) of the Right to Information Act (read with Goa RTI Rules), where the information sought concerns the life or liberty of a person, the PIO is statutorily mandated to provide the information within 48 hours of receiving the request.

### Question 679
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [GOA_ECONOMY_ADVANCED] What is the statutory capping limit on iron ore extraction in Goa per annum as per the directive of the Supreme Court of India?**

- B) 50 Million Metric Tonnes (MMT)
- C) 10 Million Metric Tonnes (MMT)

### Question 688
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q91] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 192 workers working 96 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 184320 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 10 Days
- B) 5 Days
- C) 15 Days
- D) 20 Days

**Answer:** A) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 192 × 96 = 18432 man-hours/day. Total days required = 184320 / 18432 = 10 days.

### Question 691
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [GOA_POLITY_ADVANCED] Consider the following statements regarding Article 371I of the Indian Constitution:**
- A) 1, 2, and 3
- B) 1 only
- C) 1 and 2 only
- D) 2 and 3 only

**Answer:** C) 1 and 2 only
**Explanation:** Article 371I was inserted by the 56th Constitutional Amendment Act, 1987 when Goa attained full statehood, mandating a minimum strength of 30 assembly members (though Goa currently has 40 seats). Statement 3 is incorrect as the Governor does not possess exclusive unilateral dissolution powers outside standard constitutional norms under Article 163/174.

### Question 692
**[GENERAL_KNOWLEDGE_INDIA] Which fundamental right was removed from the list of Fundamental Rights by the 44th Constitutional Amendment Act of 1978?**
- A) Right to Freedom of Speech
- B) Right to Constitutional Remedies
- C) Right to Property
- D) Right to Equality

**Answer:** C) Right to Property
**Explanation:** The 44th Amendment Act (1978) deleted the Right to Property as a Fundamental Right and made it a legal/constitutional right under Article 300A.

### Question 737
**[GOA_ADVANCED_GPSC_HARD] [GOA_HISTORY_ADVANCED] Match the following historic revolts/movements in Goa with their respective leaders:**
- A) (a)-3, (b)-1, (c)-4, (d)-2
- B) (a)-2, (b)-1, (c)-4, (d)-3
- C) (a)-1, (b)-2, (c)-3, (d)-4
- D) (a)-2, (b)-4, (c)-1, (d)-3

**Answer:** B) (a)-2, (b)-1, (c)-4, (d)-3
**Explanation:** (a) Cuncolim Revolt 1580 was led by the local Desais/Chieftains. (b) Pinto Conspiracy 1787 was led by native priests including Fr. Caetano Vitorino de Faria of Candolim. (c) June 18, 1946 movement was ignited by Dr. Ram Manohar Lohia & T.B. Cunha. (d) Rane Revolt 1852 was led by Deepaji Rane against Portuguese tax oppression.

### Question 743
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q64] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 138 workers working 69 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 95220 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 5 Days
- B) 10 Days
- C) 20 Days
- D) 15 Days

**Answer:** B) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 138 × 69 = 9522 man-hours/day. Total days required = 95220 / 9522 = 10 days.

---

### Question 784
**[QUANTITATIVE_APTITUDE] What will be the compound interest on ₹10,000 for 2 years at 10% per annum compounded annually?**
- A) ₹2,000
- B) ₹2,500
- C) ₹2,100
- D) ₹2,200

**Answer:** C) ₹2,100
**Explanation:** Amount = P(1 + r/100)^t = 10000 × (1.1)² = 10000 × 1.21 = ₹12,100.
CI = Amount - Principal = 12100 - 10000 = ₹2,100.

### Question 853
**[LOGICAL_REASONING] Pointing to a photograph, Rohit said, "She is the daughter of my grandfather's only son." How is the woman in the photograph related to Rohit?**
- A) Cousin
- B) Aunt
- C) Sister
- D) Mother

**Answer:** C) Sister
**Explanation:** Rohit's grandfather's only son is Rohit's father.
The daughter of Rohit's father is Rohit's sister.

### Question 918
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q98] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 206 workers working 103 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 212180 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 15 Days
- B) 5 Days
- C) 20 Days
- D) 10 Days

**Answer:** D) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 206 × 103 = 21218 man-hours/day. Total days required = 212180 / 21218 = 10 days.

### Question 942
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q68] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 146 workers working 73 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 106580 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 10 Days
- B) 20 Days
- C) 5 Days
- D) 15 Days

**Answer:** A) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 146 × 73 = 10658 man-hours/day. Total days required = 106580 / 10658 = 10 days.

### Question 984
**[ADVANCED_QUANT_DI] [ADVANCED_QUANT_Q15] A civil engineering project in Goa requires 40 workers working 20 hours daily. If the total project work is evaluated at 8000 man-hours, how many total days will be required to complete the infrastructure contract?**
- A) 10 Days
- B) 15 Days
- C) 20 Days
- D) 5 Days

**Answer:** A) 10 Days
**Explanation:** Total daily output = 40 × 20 = 800 man-hours/day. Total days required = 8000 / 800 = 10 days.

### Question 985
**[GITHUB_REPOS_APTITUDE] [GitHub Repo: ratneshmaurya/Aptitude_Practice_Questions] What is the HCF of 24, 36, and 60?**
- A) 6
- B) 12
- C) 24
- D) 18

**Answer:** B) 12
**Explanation:** Factors of 24 = 2³ × 3
Factors of 36 = 2² × 3²
Factors of 60 = 2² × 3 × 5
HCF = 2² × 3 = 12.

---

### Question 1022
**[GITHUB_REPOS_APTITUDE] [GitHub Repo: drsarveshwarbharti/Aptitude-For-Placements] If A:B = 2:3 and B:C = 4:5, what is A:B:C?**
- A) 8:12:15
- B) 2:4:5
- C) 8:10:15
- D) 6:9:15

**Answer:** A) 8:12:15
**Explanation:** Multiply A:B by 4 -> 8:12.
Multiply B:C by 3 -> 12:15.
Combined ratio A:B:C = 8:12:15.
